### [235] [Robust probabilistic measurement of structural-functional module consistency in infant brain development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19739)
*Lingbin Bian,Feihong Liu,Qian Wang,Han Zhang,Dinggang Shen,the UNC/UMN Baby Connectome Project Consortium*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机模块的脑网络结构-功能模块一致性（SFMC）概率测量方法，能够鲁棒地评估不同大小的结构模块与功能模块之间的一致性，并考虑个体间模块变异性。基于婴儿连接组项目（BCP）数据集的结果显示，0-5岁期间SFMC随年龄增长而下降，初级脑区（如视觉区）一致性较高，而高级认知区域（如注意、控制和默认模式网络）一致性较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统脑网络模块分析通常在群体水平上将脑网络划分为固定模块，忽略了模块的个体间变异性和结构-功能模块大小不一致的问题。本文旨在提出一种鲁棒的概率测量方法来评估结构-功能模块一致性。

Method: 引入随机模块概念，将每个脑区在群体水平上被分配到某个子网络的可能性建模为分配概率，从而实现对结构-功能模块一致性的概率化鲁棒测量。

Result: 基于BCP数据集的分析表明：SFMC在0-5岁期间呈下降趋势；初级脑区（如视觉区域）SFMC较高，而高级认知区域（如注意、控制和默认模式网络）SFMC较低；与传统的结构-功能耦合方法相比，随机模块方法揭示了更显著的结构-功能耦合下降，表明更强的发育重组。

Conclusion: 随机模块方法能够更敏感地检测脑网络发育过程中的结构-功能重组，为理解大脑发育过程中模块化组织的动态变化提供了新工具。

Original Abstract: Brain network is commonly divided into modules for analyzing their functionally segregated roles for group-level analysis in neuroimaging studies. Here, we introduce stochastic modules within brain networks for a robust probabilistic measurement of structural-functional module consistency (SFMC) in a group of subjects. Specifically, a stochastic module can be regarded as the chance of a brain region across subjects potentially being assigned to a group-level sub-network, characterized as an assignment probability for this brain region. This novel method has two advantages for evaluating inhomogeneous modules in brain networks. The first is that it can robustly evaluate the consistency between brain structural and functional modules whose population sizes are not necessary the same, and the second is that it is able to take into account the inter-individual variability of the modules for the groups. Moreover, compared with the conventional structural-functional coupling approach, our stochastic module-based method reveals a more pronounced decline in the coupling between structure and function, indicating stronger developmental reorganization. Our results using the dataset from Baby Connectome Project (BCP) show that the SFMC decreases from 0 to 5 years old, and is greater in primary brain regions, such as visual areas, while lower in more advanced cognitive regions, including those related to attention, control, and default mode network.

中文摘要翻译: 脑网络通常被划分为模块，用于在神经影像学研究的群体水平分析中探究其功能隔离角色。本文引入脑网络中的随机模块，以对一组受试者的结构-功能模块一致性（SFMC）进行鲁棒的概率测量。具体而言，随机模块可被视为一个脑区在不同受试者中可能被分配到某个群体水平子网络的机会，其特征表现为该脑区的分配概率。这种新方法在评估脑网络中非均匀模块方面具有两个优势：第一，它能够鲁棒地评估群体规模不必相同的脑结构与功能模块之间的一致性；第二，它能够考虑模块在群体中的个体间变异性。此外，与传统的结构-功能耦合方法相比，我们的基于随机模块的方法揭示了结构与功能之间更显著的耦合下降，表明更强的发育重组。我们使用婴儿连接组项目（BCP）数据集的结果显示，SFMC在0-5岁期间下降，且在初级脑区（如视觉区域）较高，而在更高级的认知区域（包括与注意、控制和默认模式网络相关的区域）较低。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Full-Self Diagnostics (FSD): Physics-Grounded Visual Biomarker Inference from Smartphone Video via Inverse Problems and Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19372)
*Jonathan Thomas,Harsh Thaker*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出全自诊断（FSD）框架，从智能手机拍摄的9秒面部视频中无创恢复潜在生理状态。该框架融合物理正向模型、信息论可观测性理论、正则化逆问题、算子学习泛化及监督学习五个互惠组件。在38812对真实世界扫描数据上验证，血糖预测MARD为29.86%，97.57%预测位于克拉克误差网格A+B区，受控糖尿病患者MARD达17%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有非侵入式生理状态监测方法通常需要专用设备或受控环境，缺乏从普通消费级设备（如智能手机）捕获的不受约束面部视频中可靠推断生物标志物的统一数学框架。

Method: 提出FSD框架，包含五个互惠组件：（1）基于辐射传输方程和发色团吸收的物理正向模型；（2）信息论可观测性理论证明多通道视觉信号与生理状态的互信息严格递增；（3）Tikhonov正则化逆问题保证域均匀可辨识性；（4）算子学习公式支持跨设备、分辨率和人群泛化；（5）可解释为随机变分推断的监督学习过程，性能随配对观测数平方根倒数比例提升。

Result: 在38812对真实配对扫描（59名受试者）上验证。首席作者自采数据（血糖范围35-550 mg/dL）MARD为29.86%，97.57%预测在克拉克误差网格A+B区，仅0.27%在危险E区。控制良好的糖尿病患者在70-180 mg/dL较窄区间MARD达17%。

Conclusion: 消费级面部视频在完全不受约束条件下编码了足够的结构化信息，可用于临床相关的非侵入式生物标志物推断，且性能随配对数据增多而可预测地提升。

Original Abstract: We present Full-Self Diagnostics (FSD), a unified mathematical framework for recovering latent physiological states from unconstrained 9-second facial videos captured by consumer smartphones. The approach integrates five mutually reinforcing components: (1) a physics-based forward model derived from the radiative transfer equation and chromophore absorption that maps camera observables to biomarker concentrations; (2) an information-theoretic observability theory proving that multi-channel visual signals (spectral, pulse, respiratory, micro-expression, and oculomotor) contain strictly increasing mutual information with physiological state; (3) a stable, Tikhonov-regularized inverse problem with domain-uniform identifiability guarantees; (4) an operator-learning formulation that enables generalization across devices, resolutions, and populations; and (5) a supervised learning procedure, interpretable as stochastic variational inference, that continuously refines the model from paired biosensor ground truth with performance improving proportionally to one over the square root of the number of paired observations.
  Empirical validation on 38812 real-world paired scans across 59 subjects demonstrates practical performance. Self-collected data from the lead author (glucose range 35-550 mg/dL) yields MARD of 29.86 percent with 97.57 percent of predictions in Clarke Error Grid Zones A+B and only 0.27 percent in the dangerous Zone E. A well-managed diabetic participant achieves MARD of 17 percent in the narrower 70-180 mg/dL band. These results confirm that consumer-grade facial video encodes sufficient structured information for clinically relevant, non-invasive biomarker inference under fully unconstrained conditions, with performance scaling predictably as more paired data becomes available.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出全自诊断（FSD），一个统一的数学框架，用于从消费级智能手机拍摄的不受约束的9秒面部视频中恢复潜在的生理状态。该方法整合了五个相互增强的组件：（1）基于辐射传输方程和发色团吸收的物理正向模型，将相机可观测变量映射为生物标志物浓度；（2）信息论可观测性理论，证明多通道视觉信号（光谱、脉搏、呼吸、微表情和眼动）与生理状态包含严格递增的互信息；（3）一个稳定的、经Tikhonov正则化的逆问题，具有域均匀的可辨识性保证；（4）一个算子学习公式，支持跨设备、分辨率和人群的泛化；（5）一个可解释为随机变分推断的监督学习过程，从配对的生物传感器真实数据中持续优化模型，其性能改善与配对观测数量的平方根成反比。对59名受试者采集的38812对真实世界配对扫描进行的实证验证展示了实际性能。首席作者自采数据（血糖范围35-550 mg/dL）的MARD为29.86%，97.57%的预测位于克拉克误差网格A+B区，仅0.27%位于危险E区。一位控制良好的糖尿病参与者在较窄的70-180 mg/dL范围内实现了17%的MARD。这些结果证实，消费级面部视频在完全不受约束的条件下编码了足够的结构化信息，可用于临床相关的非侵入式生物标志物推断，且性能随更多配对数据的可用性而可预测地提升。

</details>


### [237] [FrequencyFormer: A Co-Designed Sensor-to-Processor Pipeline for Frequency-Domain Vision Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19574)
*Chengwei Zhou,Ovishake Sen,Xuming Chen,Rishith Paramasivam,Shaahin Angizi,Swarup Bhunia,Baibhab Chatterjee,Gourav Datta*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出FrequencyFormer，一种频域令牌化方法，通过多尺度DCT压缩图像数据，结合近传感器硬件实现，实现128倍数据压缩和230倍通信能耗降低，为传感器端视觉Transformer高效部署提供可扩展方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传感器边缘系统部署ViT不仅受限于设备端计算能力，更受限于传感器到处理器间传输高维图像数据所需的能量和带宽。现有近传感器计算方法仅提供有限的数据压缩，亟需更高效的压缩方案以减少数据移动开销。

Method: (1) 多尺度DCT令牌化器：将224×224图像压缩为紧凑频域令牌，实现128倍片外数据量减少；(2) 基于查找表(LUT)的近传感器硬件实现：利用固定DCT系数实现无乘子、高能效面积效率的令牌化；(3) 改进的基于MIPI的低功耗通信架构：进一步降低传输能量。该方案可即插即用替代标准ViT分块嵌入，兼容预训练主干网络。

Result: 达到28.8 TOPS/W能效，通信能耗降低230倍，传感器侧总能耗降低2.22倍，在分类、检测和分割任务中仅带来适度精度损失。

Conclusion: 频域令牌化可作为传感器内ViT部署的可扩展基础方案，通过传感器级频域压缩有效降低数据移动开销，实现高效ViT推理。

Original Abstract: Deploying vision transformers (ViTs) on sensor-edge systems is limited not only by on-device compute, but also by the energy and bandwidth required to transmit high-dimensional image data from the sensor to the processor. While in-sensor and near-sensor computing reduce this cost through early feature extraction, existing methods often provide only modest compression. We observe that the frequency domain provides a naturally compact representation of visual information and can be exploited at the sensor level to reduce sensor-to-processor data movement. Building on this insight, we present FrequencyFormer, a co-designed sensor-to-processor pipeline for efficient ViT inference. FrequencyFormer includes: (1) a multi-scale DCT tokenizer that compresses a 224x224 image into compact frequency-domain tokens, achieving up to 128x reduction in off-chip data volume with modest accuracy loss; (2) a LUT-based near-sensor hardware implementation that leverages fixed DCT coefficients for multiplier-free, energy- and area-efficient tokenization; and (3) a modified MIPI-based low-power communication architecture that further reduces transfer energy. FrequencyFormer serves as a drop-in replacement for standard ViT patch embedding and remains compatible with pretrained backbones across classification, detection, and segmentation tasks. The pipeline achieves 28.8 TOPS/W, reduces communication energy by 230x, and lowers total sensor-side energy by 2.22x, demonstrating frequency-domain tokenization as a scalable foundation for in-sensor ViT deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 在传感器边缘系统上部署视觉Transformer（ViT）不仅受限于设备端计算能力，还受限于从传感器向处理器传输高维图像数据所需的能量和带宽。虽然传感器内和近传感器计算通过早期特征提取降低了这一开销，但现有方法通常仅提供适度的压缩。我们观察到频域提供了视觉信息的自然紧凑表示，并可在传感器层面加以利用以减少传感器到处理器的数据移动。基于这一洞察，我们提出了FrequencyFormer——一种用于高效ViT推理的传感器到处理器协同设计流水线。FrequencyFormer包括：(1) 多尺度DCT令牌化器，将224×224图像压缩为紧凑的频域令牌，在适度精度损失下实现高达128倍的片外数据量缩减；(2) 基于查找表（LUT）的近传感器硬件实现，利用固定DCT系数实现无乘子、高能效和高面积效率的令牌化；(3) 改进的基于MIPI的低功耗通信架构，进一步降低传输能量。FrequencyFormer可作为标准ViT分块嵌入的即插即用替代方案，与分类、检测和分割任务中的预训练主干网络保持兼容。该流水线达到28.8 TOPS/W能效，通信能耗降低230倍，传感器侧总能耗降低2.22倍，展示了频域令牌化作为传感器内ViT部署的可扩展基础方案。

</details>


### [238] [Contour-Constrained Deformable Registration with Parameter Characterization for Head and Neck Surgical Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19767)
*Qingyun Yang,Jon S. Heiselman,Ayberk Acar,Morgan J. Ringel,Michael I. Miga,Matthieu Chabanas,Michael C. Topf,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于力学驱动的可变形配准框架，用于在头颈癌手术中矫正切除后组织变形，从而精准定位术中切缘阳性区域。实验表明，该方法在舌部等最具挑战性的组织样本上误差降低了49.41%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 头颈鳞状细胞癌复发率高，术中冰冻切片分析虽为标准方法，但切除后组织收缩变形导致阳性切缘在切除床上的精确定位极为困难，现有刚性配准方法无法解决这一问题。

Method: 提出基于正则化Kelvinlet基函数的可变形配准框架：将3D标本网格与术中切除床点云进行配准，同时匹配表面点云、基准标记点以及边界轮廓约束（直接惩罚标本与切除床边界之间的垂直距离偏差）。

Result: 在皮肤、颊黏膜和舌部共9个标本上：刚性配准平均目标配准误差为11.11±4.07 mm；无轮廓约束的可变形配准降至8.20±2.68 mm（降低26.19%）；引入轮廓约束后进一步降至5.62±2.28 mm（降低49.41%）。舌部样本改善最显著。

Conclusion: 轮廓约束在侧向变形较大的组织类型中主导配准精度，且算法在广泛的参数组合范围内均能稳定工作，表明该方法具有临床实用潜力。

Original Abstract: With 890,000 annual new cases globally, head and neck squamous cell carcinoma has one of the highest recurrence rates among solid malignancies. Although frozen section analysis is the standard of care for intraoperative margin assessment, accurately relocating detected positive margins on the resection bed remains challenging due to imprecise alignment between resected specimens and their resection bed, compounded by post-resection mucosal tissue shrinkage. We present a biomechanics-driven deformable registration framework that corrects post-resection tissue deformation to provide intraoperative guidance. Our approach registers 3D specimen meshes to intraoperative resection bed point clouds using a deformable registration approach based on regularized Kelvinlet basis functions. The registration matches surface point clouds, fiducial landmarks, and boundary contour constraints that directly penalize perpendicular distance-to-agreement between specimen and resection bed boundaries. Across nine specimens from skin, buccal mucosa, and tongue sites, the overall mean target registration error was $11.11 \pm 4.07$ mm using rigid registration, which decreased to $8.20 \pm 2.68$ mm (26.19\% reduction) using deformable registration without contour constraint. The proposed contour-constrained deformable registration further reduced the error to $5.62 \pm 2.28$ mm, a 49.41\% reduction relative to rigid registration. We observed the largest reduction in the most clinically challenging tongue specimens. We also performed a systematic two-stage parameter search to characterize the relative importance of surface alignment, fiducial correspondences, contour constraint, and strain energy regularization. This search revealed that contour weighting dominates registration accuracy for tissue types with large lateral deformation, while the algorithm operates over a broad range of parameter combinations.

中文摘要翻译: 头颈鳞状细胞癌全球每年新发病例89万例，是实体恶性肿瘤中复发率最高的癌种之一。虽然冰冻切片分析是术中切缘评估的标准方法，但由于切除标本与其切除床之间的对准不精确，加上切除后黏膜组织收缩，准确重新定位检测到的阳性切缘位置仍然具有挑战性。我们提出了一种基于生物力学驱动的可变形配准框架，用于矫正切除后组织变形以提供术中导航。该方法采用基于正则化Kelvinlet基函数的可变形配准方法，将3D标本网格与术中切除床点云进行配准。该配准同时匹配表面点云、基准标记点以及边界轮廓约束——直接惩罚标本边界与切除床边界之间的垂直距离偏差。在来自皮肤、颊黏膜和舌部部位的9个标本上，刚性配准的总体平均目标配准误差为11.11±4.07 mm；无轮廓约束的可变形配准将误差降至8.20±2.68 mm（降低26.19%）；本文提出的轮廓约束可变形配准进一步将误差降至5.62±2.28 mm，相比刚性配准降低了49.41%。我们观察到在最具临床挑战性的舌部标本中改善幅度最大。我们还通过系统的两阶段参数搜索，表征了表面配准、基准点对应、轮廓约束和应变能正则化的相对重要性。该搜索表明，对于侧向变形较大的组织类型，轮廓权重主导配准精度，而算法在广泛的参数组合范围内均能有效运行。

</details>


### [239] [SIMBA: ABidirectional Retrieval Forward Simulation Framework for Modeling FY-4A GIIRS Hyperspectral Infrared Radiances Toward NWP Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19943)
*Jingdong Shen,Fu Wang*,Qifeng Lu,Hao Huang,Chunqiang Wu,Chi Yang,Xiaofang Liu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMBA框架，通过双向检索-前向模拟和循环一致性约束，联合进行大气廓线反演和辐射重建，在FY-4A GIIRS高光谱红外数据上优于多个深度学习基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习方法主要关注从辐射到大气廓线的单向反演，忽视了反向辐射模拟过程以及大气状态空间与辐射观测空间之间的一致性。

Method: 提出SIMBA框架，包含双向检索-前向模拟过程，引入循环一致性约束增强两个过程的耦合，并采用双向Mamba状态空间模块沿气压层捕获长程依赖关系。

Result: 在温度反演、比湿反演、长波辐射重建和中波辐射重建任务上，SIMBA优于多个代表性深度学习基线；消融实验验证了双向设计和循环一致性机制的有效性。

Conclusion: SIMBA框架能有效联合进行大气廓线反演和高光谱红外辐射建模，未来可扩展至雅可比相关分析和NWP应用。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral infrared observations are an important data source for numerical weather prediction (NWP) because they provide rich information on the vertical structure of atmospheric temperature and humidity. However, most existing deep learning methods mainly focus on one-way retrieval from radiances to atmospheric profiles, while the reverse radiance simulation process and the consistency between atmospheric state space and radiance observation space are insufficiently considered. In this study, we propose SIMBA, a unified bidirectional retrieval-forward simulation framework for FY-4A GIIRS hyperspectral infrared radiance modeling toward NWP applications. The framework jointly performs atmospheric profile retrieval and radiance reconstruction, introduces a cycle-consistency constraint to strengthen the coupling between the two processes, and employs a bidirectional Mamba state-space module to capture long-range dependencies along pressure levels. Using collocated FY-4A GIIRS observations and ERA5 reanalysis data, the proposed method is evaluated for temperature retrieval, specific humidity retrieval, long-wave radiance reconstruction, and medium-wave radiance reconstruction. Experimental results show that SIMBA outperforms several representative deep learning baselines across both retrieval and reconstruction tasks, while ablation experiments confirm the contribution of the bidirectional design and cycle-consistency mechanism. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework is effective for joint atmospheric profile retrieval and hyperspectral infrared radiance modeling, and suggest potential for future Jacobian-related analysis and NWP-oriented extensions.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱红外观测是数值天气预报（NWP）的重要数据源，因为它们提供了大气温度和湿度垂直结构的丰富信息。然而，现有深度学习方法主要关注从辐射到大气廓线的单向反演，而反向辐射模拟过程以及大气状态空间与辐射观测空间之间的一致性未得到充分考虑。在本研究中，我们提出SIMBA，一个面向NWP应用的FY-4A GIIRS高光谱红外辐射建模的统一双向检索-前向模拟框架。该框架联合进行大气廓线反演和辐射重建，引入循环一致性约束以增强两个过程之间的耦合，并采用双向Mamba状态空间模块沿气压层捕获长程依赖关系。利用配准的FY-4A GIIRS观测数据和ERA5再分析数据，对所提方法在温度反演、比湿反演、长波辐射重建和中波辐射重建任务上进行了评估。实验结果表明，SIMBA在反演和重建任务上均优于多个代表性深度学习基线，同时消融实验证实了双向设计和循环一致性机制的贡献。这些结果表明，所提框架能有效联合进行大气廓线反演和高光谱红外辐射建模，并展示了未来在雅可比相关分析和面向NWP扩展方面的潜力。

</details>
